# Plan 4: Finalize Spec Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Turn a plumbed plan into the final spec. By the end:
- **The Finalize page** shows a checklist: what blocks Finalize, what will use a default, and what nobody reviewed.
- **Finalize spec** sends a request to the listening Claude window. A **finalizer** subagent then writes the final document in the structure `outputs/finalize.md` defines.
- **Diagrams and schema blocks** in the final are generated by the service from the drawing data: Mermaid flowcharts, sequence diagrams, numbered user-flow steps, exact schema diffs and mockup links. They're never written by hand.
- **You preview** the proposal, with a diff against the last final, then **Accept**. That saves `docs/final.md` in the plumbing project and copies it into the repo as `<name>.final.md`, with mockups in `<name>.assets/`, next to the plan. The project moves to Finalized, and the app suggests the next command.
- **Settings → Repos** gains **Detect again** for a repo profile.

**Architecture:**
- **Core** (`packages/core`) adds:
  - the finalize checklist;
  - a small Mermaid and export module that turns drawing data into Markdown blocks;
  - the finalize request lifecycle (request, pick up, proposal, accept, discard), stored in the project folder;
  - the finalizer's context pack;
  - the export into a clone, with strict path containment.
- **The service** queues a finalize request like a submission. `dp_wait` hands it to a listening window, the finalizer reads its pack through `dp_context` and sends the document back through a new `dp_finalize` tool, and the browser routes show the checklist, the proposal and Accept.
- **The web app** gains a Finalize page. The header's **Finalize spec** button opens it, or is disabled with the reason. A small **Detect again** control goes in Settings.

**Tech Stack:** Plans 1–3's stack (TypeScript 5.9, Node 22, pnpm 10, Zod 3.25, Hono 4, React 19, Vite 7, Tailwind 4, TanStack Router and Query, Vitest 3, Playwright, `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk` 1.31, `diff` 9, `elkjs`, `@tailwindcss/browser`). No new dependencies.

**Spec:** `SPEC.md` (repo root). Read §6.3, §7, §8.4, §10.2, §10.5, §10.7, §11, §13.3 and §18 step 5 before starting. `docs/how-it-works.md` explains how the plugin, the service and the app fit together.

## What Plans 1–3 left in place

Plan 3 is merged on `main`. This plan builds on:
- **Project model.**
  - `PlumbingProject` (`core/src/schemas/project.ts`) already has `docs.final?`, `docs.exportedTo?` and `status: 'importing' | 'active' | 'finalized'`.
  - The app home already has a **Finalized** tab.
  - The Draft's Final document says "No final version yet. Finalize spec creates it."
  - The header has a disabled **Finalize spec** button (`web/src/pages/ProjectHeader.tsx`).
- **Output rules.** `defaults/outputs/finalize.md` holds the final document's 14-section structure and rules. Setup copies it to `~/.dev-plumbing/outputs/finalize.md`, which the user may edit. `LoadedConfig.outputs` lists the file names, and `agents.json` already has `models.finalizer` (default `"opus"`).
- **The listening loop.**
  - `dp_wait` (`service/src/routes/claude.ts` `/wait`) picks up submissions under the project lock, hands them to a window and requeues work from windows that went away (`core/src/store/queue.ts`).
  - Listeners know which windows are waiting.
  - SKILL.md tells the main window what to do with each `dp_wait` result kind.
- **Drawing data** is validated per screen (`core/src/schemas/data.ts`: `parseData`, `DiagramData`, `TableDiff`, `MockupData`, `FlowData`, `PhaseData`).
  - Mockups are body markup in `data.after` / `data.before`.
  - Read-time checks are computed by `createDataChecker` (`core/src/store/checks.ts`).
  - `service/src/checker.ts` has `cloneOf(ctx, ref)`.
- **Repo profiles.**
  - The repo-setup agent saves a new profile through `dp_repo_profile` (`/api/claude/repo-profile`).
  - It may never set `projectsFolder` or `linkIntoClones`, and it never overwrites an existing profile.
  - Settings → Repos edits profiles (`web/src/pages/SettingsPage.tsx`, `RepoEditor`).
- **Safety:**
  - The only write into a clone so far is the `linkIntoClones` symlink.
  - `verifyCodeRefs` and `buildKitCss` show the containment patterns (realpath for kit files).
  - All writes go through `writeFileAtomic` / `writeJsonAtomic`.

## Decisions this plan makes

Review these. Each one is the plan's reading of the spec where the spec leaves room.

1. **Finalize runs through the listening window, like a submission.**
   - **Start** saves a finalize request in the project folder.
   - A listening window picks it up through `dp_wait` (`kind: 'finalize'`) and starts one `dev-plumbing:finalizer` subagent with `models.finalizer`.
   - If no window is listening, the request waits, and the app says so with Plan 2's "No Claude window is listening" wording.
   - A window that goes away puts the request back in the queue.
   - A finalizer that returns without sending a proposal marks the request failed, with the reason, and **Try again**.
2. **The finalizer never draws.** It places tokens, and the service replaces each one with a block generated from the item's data. So Mermaid is always valid and always matches what you agreed. The tokens are:
   - `{{diagram:<itemId>}}`: a Mermaid flowchart;
   - `{{sequence:<itemId>}}`: a Mermaid sequence diagram, for system and both flows;
   - `{{steps:<itemId>}}`: numbered user-flow steps;
   - `{{schema:<itemId>}}`: the exact schema diff, as a `diff` block;
   - `{{migration:<itemId>}}`: the migration notes, as a list;
   - `{{mockup:<itemId>:after|before}}`: a link into `<name>.assets/`.

   A proposal with an unknown token, a token for a parked item, or a token whose item has no such data, is refused with every problem listed, as Plan 2's replies are.
3. **What blocks Finalize** (§10.5, §11's gate):
   - a question with `blocking: true` that isn't resolved;
   - a concern with `severity` critical or high that isn't resolved;
   - any thread with Claude;
   - any thread whose latest Claude options are waiting for your answer and carry a change (a proposal);
   - any small edit that is pending, not applied.

   Parked items don't block; they're listed as "Parked: left out of the final". Unanswered non-blocking questions with a `default` are listed under "These will use their default". Items whose threads never had a message from you are a warning ("Nobody has reviewed these").
4. **A proposal belongs to the draft it was written from.**
   - The proposal stores a hash of everything the final is built from: the draft, every item (with its drawing data) and the active decisions.
   - If any of them changes before you accept, Accept is disabled with "The draft changed since Claude wrote this. Finalize again." That includes an accepted redraw that changes only a diagram or a table, since that changes the generated Mermaid or schema block.
   - So you never accept a final that misses a later decision.
5. **Accept writes four things, and nothing else:**
   - `docs/final.md`. Any previous final is first moved to `finals/<timestamp>.md`, so earlier finals stay in dev-plumbing's history.
   - `<name>.final.md`, next to the plan in the chosen clone.
   - `<name>.assets/<itemId>.after.html` and `.before.html` for every UI item with markup.
   - `project.json` (status, exportedTo).

   The clone must be a git clone whose remote matches the repo profile. Every target goes through realpath containment inside that clone, and a symlinked `.final.md` or assets folder is refused. Assets this project wrote before and no longer needs are removed; nothing else in that folder is touched.
   - The project keeps the mockups as item data, not files: SPEC §7's `docs/final.assets/` isn't written. The final's mockup links work in the repo copy, next to `<name>.assets/`.
6. **Mockup assets are plain HTML files.**
   - Each is a minimal document with the body markup.
   - A comment at the top names the app, route and kit files.
   - There is no script, and no compiler: the classes are the app's own, so an implementer reads them as code.
7. **The app's preview shows Mermaid as code blocks.** GitHub and most editors render them, and bundling a Mermaid renderer isn't worth its size here.
8. **The project remembers every clone it was opened from.** `project.json` gains `clones`, and Accept offers them, the source clone first.
9. **Detect again**:
   - **Requesting it:** Settings → Repos → **Detect again** records a request for that repo.
   - **Running it:** the next listening window on any project of that repo runs the repo-setup subagent (`dp_wait` `kind: 'detect-profile'`), or the next `/dev-plumbing` in a clone of it does (`dp_open` `kind: 'needs-profile'` with `redetect: true`).
   - **What it may change:** it may replace `planFolders`, `schema`, `conventions`, `apps` and `sensitiveData`. It never changes `name`, `match`, `projectsFolder` or `linkIntoClones`.
   - **Settings afterwards** show when it last ran.
10. **After Accept, the project stays Finalized.**
    - Later answers don't move it back; the Finalize page shows how many changes there have been since the last final, and **Finalize again** replaces both copies after a new preview.
    - The suggested next command is `writing-plans <path of the .final.md in the repo>`, with a copy button.

## Global Constraints

Everything in Plans 1–3's Global Constraints still applies:
- Node `>=22.12`, pnpm `10.x`, TypeScript `strict`, ESM.
- The public repo stays generic: examples use the made-up "Acme" app, with no real company names, paths or emails.
- `~/.dev-plumbing/`, overridable with `DEV_PLUMBING_HOME`. Never overwrite a user's config file except through an explicit **Reset to default** or, new in this plan, a **Detect again** the user asked for (Decision 9).
- Atomic writes. The service is the only writer of the projects folder, always under the project's lock.
- Subagents get only `Read`, `Grep`, `Glob` and their dp tools.
- The service binds `127.0.0.1` with the Host, token and same-origin guard.
- The Ink wash theme exactly per §16:
  - colour only as dots, text and thin lines;
  - no tinted boxes;
  - tokens only;
  - one primary button per screen;
  - sentence-case copy;
  - one column under 768 px with no sideways scrolling.
- Tests clean up their temp folders (`tempDir` and `removeTempDirs`). Tests never touch the real `~/.dev-plumbing`, never run the real `launchctl`, never install plugins, and never use port 4545. e2e runs on 45459 and the smoke test on 45461.
- Every commit ends with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`, passed as a second `-m`, whatever model wrote it.
- Work in a git worktree, never in the main checkout, which runs the user's live app and plugin.

New in this plan:
- **The only writes into a clone** are the `linkIntoClones` symlink and Accept's `<name>.final.md` and `<name>.assets/`, next to the plan. The plan in the repo is never edited.
- **The finalizer's document reaches disk only through `dp_finalize`**, which validates and expands tokens. Accept only copies what you previewed.
- **Mermaid, schema blocks and mockup links in the final are generated by core** (`core/src/finalExport.ts`), never written by a subagent.
- **Names:**
  - agent `finalizer`;
  - tool `dp_finalize`;
  - `dp_wait` kinds `finalize` and `detect-profile`;
  - files `finalize.json`, `docs/final.proposed.md`, `docs/final.md` and `finals/<timestamp>.md` in the project folder;
  - `run/detect.json` in the config folder.
- **Exact copy, used verbatim:**
  - "Finalize spec"
  - "Finalize again"
  - "Start finalize"
  - "These block Finalize"
  - "These will use their default"
  - "Parked: left out of the final"
  - "Nobody has reviewed these"
  - "Waiting for Claude to write the final."
  - "Claude is writing the final."
  - "No Claude window is listening. Run /dev-plumbing in any clone."
  - "The finalizer didn't send a final."
  - "Try again"
  - "Preview"
  - "Changes since the last final"
  - "The draft changed since Claude wrote this. Finalize again."
  - "Accept"
  - "Discard"
  - "Copy into"
  - "Finalized"
  - "Next"
  - "Detect again"
  - "Detection runs the next time a Claude window for this repo listens, or you run /dev-plumbing in a clone."
  - "1 item blocks Finalize" / "N items block Finalize"
  - "Nothing blocks Finalize."
  - "Default: <value>"
  - "N changes since the last final." / "No changes since the last final."
  - "Copied to <path>"
  - "Copy" / "Copied"
  - "Last detected <when>"
  - "Detection requested"
  - "Discard this final? Finalize again writes a new one."
  - "None of the clones this project was opened from is on this Mac."
  - "That folder isn't one of the clones this project was opened from."
  - "Wait for the import to finish, then finalize."
  - `<token>: "<title>" is parked, so it's left out of the final.`

## Review Focus

These five situations aren't the main path, but they're the most likely to hurt someone using this. Each has a test in the task named.

1. **Accept writing outside the right place.** A chosen clone that isn't a clone of this repo, a plan path or assets folder that is a symlink pointing elsewhere, or an existing `<name>.final.md` that is a symlink: none of these is written through. Accept refuses with a clear message and writes nothing anywhere, including `docs/final.md`. Tested in Task 4 ("accept never writes outside the chosen clone").
2. **Accepting a stale proposal.** If any answer, accept or small edit changes the draft, an item or its drawing data after the finalizer wrote its proposal, Accept is refused and the page says so. Tested in Task 4 ("a proposal for an older draft can't be accepted") and Task 10 (e2e).
3. **A finalizer that invents.**
   - A proposal using a token for an item that doesn't exist, or a `{{sequence:…}}` for a user-only flow, is refused as a whole with every problem listed, and nothing is saved.
   - Any `{{` the finalizer writes that isn't a valid token is refused, so no unexpanded token reaches a saved proposal.

   Tested in Task 2 ("unknown or mismatched tokens are refused") and Task 3 ("a refused proposal saves nothing").
4. **A window that goes away mid-finalize, or a finalizer that gives up.** The request never stays stuck on "Claude is writing the final." A dead window's request goes back to the queue. A window that reports back with no proposal marks it failed, with **Try again**. Tested in Task 3 ("a finalize request survives a window that went away") and Task 6 (the service round trip).
5. **Running Finalize again.**
   - The previous final is kept in `finals/`.
   - The repo copy is replaced only after Accept.
   - Assets this project no longer needs are removed.
   - A file the user added to the assets folder survives.

   Tested in Task 4 ("finalizing again keeps history and cleans only our assets").

## Contracts

The exact names and types the tasks share. A task's implementer sees only their own task, so every cross-task name is fixed here.

### Core: `schemas/finalize.ts` (Task 1; Task 3 adds `requeuedAt` and `proposal.assets`)

Exported from `@dev-plumbing/core/schemas`.

```ts
export const finalizeStateValues = ['requested', 'writing', 'proposed', 'failed'] as const;
export type FinalizeState = (typeof finalizeStateValues)[number];
/** finalize.json in the project folder. Absent when no finalize is under way. */
export type FinalizeRequest = {
  id: string;                     // newId('f')
  state: FinalizeState;
  requestedAt: string;
  pickedUpAt?: string; pickedUpBy?: string;   // window id
  requeuedAt?: string;            // (added by Task 3) set when a dead window's request went back in the queue
  proposal?: {
    at: string;
    draftHash: string;            // finalInputsHash(dir) when the proposal was saved (Task 3)
    file: 'docs/final.proposed.md';
    length: number;
    assets: { itemId: string; side: 'after' | 'before' }[];   // (added by Task 3) the mockups the final links to, from expandTokens
  };
  failedAt?: string; reason?: string;
};
export const finalizeRequestSchema: z.ZodType<FinalizeRequest>;

export type ChecklistEntry = { itemId: string; threadId: string; title: string; typeTitle: string; reason: string };
export type FinalizeChecklist = {
  blocking: ChecklistEntry[];      // "These block Finalize"
  defaults: (ChecklistEntry & { defaultValue: string })[];   // "These will use their default"
  parked: ChecklistEntry[];        // "Parked: left out of the final"
  unreviewed: ChecklistEntry[];    // "Nobody has reviewed these" (warning)
  canStart: boolean;               // blocking.length === 0
};
```

`schemas/project.ts`: `plumbingProjectSchema` gains `clones: z.array(z.string()).default([])`. `exportedTo` gains `assets: z.array(z.string()).default([])`, the asset file names this project wrote.

### Core: checklist (Task 1)

```ts
// store/checklist.ts
export async function finalizeChecklist(dir: string, types: PlumbingType[]): Promise<FinalizeChecklist>;
/** The same, from data already read (the project home and the Finalize view use it). */
export function checklistFrom(o: { items: Item[]; threads: Thread[]; history: HistoryEntry[]; types: PlumbingType[] }): FinalizeChecklist;
/** History entries applied, and not undone, after exportedAt; 0 without a final. */
export function changesSinceFinal(history: HistoryEntry[], exportedAt: string | undefined): number;
// store/open.ts
/** Adds a clone to project.clones (source clone first, tildified, no repeats). Callers hold the project lock. */
export async function recordClone(dir: string, clone: string, home?: string): Promise<string[]>;
// store/io.ts: projectFiles(dir).finalize is `<dir>/finalize.json`.
// store/finalize.ts: Task 1 creates it with readFinalize only; Task 3 replaces it whole.
```

Each item appears in at most one list: parked, then blocking, then nothing for a resolved item, then defaults, then unreviewed. Parked always wins. A pending small edit blocks even on a resolved thread. Blocking and severity are read from `fields.blocking === 'true'` and `fields.severity`, on any plumbing type. The importer's opening options never count as "a proposal is waiting". Items of disabled plumbing types are left out.

Exact `reason` strings:
- "Blocking question, not resolved."
- "High-severity concern, not resolved."
- "Claude is working on it."
- "A proposal is waiting for your answer."
- "A small edit is waiting to be applied."
- "Parked."
- "No answer yet; the default will be used."
- "Nobody has answered here."

### Core: `finalExport.ts` (Task 2)

```ts
export function diagramMermaid(d: DiagramData): string;      // fenced ```mermaid flowchart LR block; subgraph per group; dashed -.-> for style dashed; status as a class (classDef new/changed/unchanged/external)
export function sequenceMermaid(f: FlowData): string;       // fenced ```mermaid sequenceDiagram; autonumber; participants from lanes; notes for steps without from/to
export function userFlowSteps(f: FlowData): string;         // numbered Markdown list "1. label" + "   - System: systemNote"
export function schemaBlock(t: TableDiff): string;          // fenced ```diff block of schemaDiff
export function migrationList(t: TableDiff): string;        // "- **Rollback:** text" lines, kind labels as on the Database screen
export function mockupAssetName(itemId: string, side: 'after' | 'before'): string;   // `${itemId}.${side}.html`
export function mockupAssetHtml(o: { title: string; app: string; route?: string; kitFiles: string[]; body: string }): string;
export const TOKEN_PATTERN: RegExp;                          // /\{\{(diagram|sequence|steps|schema|migration|mockup):([a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*)(?::(after|before))?\}\}/g
export type TokenContext = { items: Item[]; types: PlumbingType[]; assetsDir: string };   // assetsDir is the relative folder name, `<name>.assets`
/** Replaces every token. Problems name each bad token; any problem means no output. */
export function expandTokens(markdown: string, ctx: TokenContext): { ok: true; markdown: string; assets: { itemId: string; side: 'after' | 'before' }[] } | { ok: false; problems: string[] };
/** The token list a finalizer may use for these items, one line each, for its context pack. */
export function availableTokens(items: Item[], types: PlumbingType[]): string[];
```

- **Mermaid labels** are put on one line and escaped: `"` → `#quot;`, `` ` `` → `#96;`, `;` → `#59;`, `%` → `#37;`, `<` → `#lt;`, `>` → `#gt;`. Sequence participant aliases are escaped, not quoted, because Mermaid keeps the quotes.
- **Ids** are prefixed (`n_`, `g_`, `l_`), and every character but letters, digits and `_` becomes `_`. Clashes get `_2`, `_3`.
- **Block tokens** (every kind but `mockup`) must be alone on their line: "put this token on a line of its own.". The block is indented to match the token.
- **Any leftover `{{`** in the finalizer's own text that `TOKEN_PATTERN` doesn't match is a problem: "Unknown token: {{…}}."
- **`availableTokens`** skips items whose id can't be named by a token (not `[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*`).

### Core: finalize lifecycle (Task 3)

```ts
// store/finalize.ts
export async function readFinalize(dir: string): Promise<FinalizeRequest | null>;
export async function requestFinalize(dir: string, o: { types: PlumbingType[]; now?: Date }): Promise<FinalizeRequest>;          // throws ConflictError with the blocking reasons when !canStart, or when one is already requested or writing
export async function pickUpFinalize(dir: string, windowId: string, now?: Date): Promise<FinalizeRequest | null>;                 // requested → writing
export async function saveProposal(dir: string, o: { requestId: string; markdown: string; types: PlumbingType[]; name: string; now?: Date }): Promise<FinalizeRequest>;   // validates + expands (expandTokens), writes docs/final.proposed.md, records draftHash; InputError via nothingSaved on problems
export async function finishFinalize(dir: string, o: { requestId: string; windowId: string; now?: Date }): Promise<void>;          // writing without proposal → failed, reason "The finalizer didn't send a final."
export async function requeueFinalize(dir: string, isAlive: (windowId: string) => boolean, now?: Date): Promise<boolean>;          // writing by a dead window → requested
export async function discardProposal(dir: string): Promise<void>;                                                                 // removes finalize.json and docs/final.proposed.md
export function draftHash(text: string): string;                                                                                   // sha256 hex
/** sha256 hex over the draft, every item (keys sorted, flags left out) and the active decisions: what the final is built from. */
export async function finalInputsHash(dir: string): Promise<string>;
/** The plan file's name without extension, used for <name>.final.md and <name>.assets. */
export function finalName(sourcePath: string): string;
export const PROPOSAL_FILE = 'docs/final.proposed.md';
```

- **`requestFinalize`** refuses with ConflictError:
  - "Wait for the import to finish, then finalize." while the project is importing;
  - "Finalize is already under way." while a request is `requested` or `writing`;
  - `Finalize is blocked:\n- <title>: <reason>` (one line per blocking entry) when the checklist can't start.
- **`saveProposal`** refuses an unknown or finished request with ConflictError "There's no finalize request <id> waiting for a final.". It refuses an empty or over-long final, or any token problem, with InputError through `nothingSaved`, saving nothing. It stores `proposal.draftHash = await finalInputsHash(dir)`.
  - Tokens can name only items that go into the final: it passes `expandTokens` the items that aren't parked and whose plumbing type is enabled.
  - A token naming a parked item gets the problem `<token>: "<title>" is parked, so it's left out of the final.`; one naming an item of a disabled type gets the usual `<token>: there's no item "<id>".`
- **A proposal is stale** when `(await finalInputsHash(dir)) !== proposal.draftHash`. Tasks 4 and 6 both use exactly this.

### Core: accept (Task 4)

```ts
// store/accept.ts
export async function acceptFinal(o: { dir: string; clone: string; profile: RepoProfile; types: PlumbingType[]; home?: string; now?: Date }):
  Promise<{ exportedTo: { clone: string; path: string; at: string; assets: string[] }; nextCommand: string }>;
```

It refuses (ConflictError or InputError) when:
- there's no proposal;
- the draft hash differs, with "The draft changed since Claude wrote this. Finalize again.";
- the clone isn't a directory, isn't a git clone, or its remote doesn't match the profile;
- any target escapes the clone, or is or goes through a symlink.

Every check runs before the first write. The writes come in this order:
1. Archive the old final.
2. Write `docs/final.md`.
3. Write the repo copy.
4. Write the assets, and remove the stale ones.
5. Write `project.json`: `docs.final`, `exportedTo`, `status: 'finalized'`.
6. Remove `finalize.json` and the proposal.

`nextCommand` is `` `writing-plans ${relPathOfFinalInRepo}` ``.
- `exportedTo.path` is relative to the clone, and `exportedTo.clone` is the clone, tildified with `home` (the service passes `ctx.home`, as `recordClone` gets; `~` paths are expanded with it too). The app shows "Copied to `${clone}/${path}`".
- The repo copy is byte for byte the previewed `docs/final.proposed.md`.
- The assets written are exactly `proposal.assets`, named with `mockupAssetName`.
- Clone and place problems are InputError (400). No proposal, a stale proposal or a changed mockup is ConflictError (409). A failure part-way is ConflictError, with a message saying what was put back.

### Core: context pack (Task 5)

```ts
// store/context.ts
export type FinalizePack = {
  project: { repo: string; id: string; title: string; sourcePath: string; name: string };
  rules: string;                       // outputs/finalize.md, read by the service from the config folder
  draft: string;
  items: { id: string; type: string; typeTitle: string; title: string; summary: string; body: string | null; fields: Record<string, string>; status: DisplayStatus; codeRefs: CodeRef[]; dataSummary: string | null }[];
  decisions: { text: string; itemId: string | null; itemTitle: string | null; chosen: string | null; rejected: string[]; why: string | null }[];
  defaults: { itemId: string; title: string; defaultValue: string }[];
  openItems: { itemId: string; title: string; typeTitle: string }[];      // non-blocking, unresolved
  conventions: string[];
  tokens: string[];                    // availableTokens(...)
  previousFinal: string | null;
};
export async function finalizePack(o: { dir: string; types: PlumbingType[]; profile?: RepoProfile; rules: string }): Promise<FinalizePack>;
```

### Core: detect (Task 8)

```ts
// store/detect.ts
export function detectFilePath(configDir: string): string;                                            // <configDir>/run/detect.json, { [repo]: { requestedAt?, lastDetectedAt? } }
export async function requestDetect(configDir: string, repo: string, now?: Date): Promise<void>;     // run/detect.json { [repo]: { requestedAt } }
export async function pendingDetect(configDir: string, repo: string): Promise<boolean>;
export async function finishDetect(configDir: string, repo: string, now?: Date): Promise<void>;      // removes the request, records lastDetectedAt
export async function lastDetected(configDir: string): Promise<Record<string, string>>;              // repo → ISO date
/** The detected profile replaces only planFolders, schema, conventions, apps and sensitiveData. */
export function mergeDetected(existing: RepoProfile, detected: RepoProfile): RepoProfile;
```

### Views (Tasks 1, 6)

```ts
// ProjectHome gains:
finalize: { canStart: boolean; blockingCount: number; state: FinalizeState | null; changesSinceFinal: number };
// New, for the Finalize page (GET /api/projects/:repo/:id/finalize):
export type FinalizeView = {
  checklist: FinalizeChecklist;
  request: FinalizeRequest | null;
  proposal: { markdown: string; stale: boolean; diff: DiffSegment[] | null } | null;   // stale: finalInputsHash(dir) !== proposal.draftHash; diff against docs/final.md when one exists
  final: { exportedTo: NonNullable<PlumbingProject['docs']['exportedTo']>; nextCommand: string } | null;   // nextCommand: `writing-plans ${exportedTo.path}`
  clones: { path: string; source: boolean }[];   // source clone first; only clones that exist
  name: string;                                  // finalName(source.path)
  listening: ListeningState;
  changesSinceFinal: number;
};
```

### Service (Tasks 6, 8)

- **Browser routes** (`routes/finalize.ts`, mounted under `/api`):
  - `GET /projects/:repo/:id/finalize` returns `FinalizeView`.
  - `POST /projects/:repo/:id/finalize` (start) returns `{ request, listening, message }`. The message is "Waiting for Claude to write the final." / "No Claude window is listening. Run /dev-plumbing in any clone." It notifies listeners. A blocked start returns 409 with the reasons.
  - `POST /projects/:repo/:id/finalize/accept` takes `{ clone: string }` and returns the `acceptFinal` result.
    - The profile is `cfg.repos.find(p => p.name === ref.repo)`, with 400 "There's no repo profile for <repo>. Add it in Settings → Repos." if it's missing.
    - A clone that isn't in `FinalizeView.clones` is refused with 400 "That folder isn't one of the clones this project was opened from.".
    - It runs under the project lock, then calls `changed(ref)`.
  - `POST /projects/:repo/:id/finalize/discard` returns `{ ok: true }`.
  - Start, accept and discard all call `changed(ref)`. The start route takes no body. Its two messages are exported as `FINALIZE_WAITING` and `FINALIZE_NO_WINDOW`.
- **Claude routes:**
  - `/wait` hands out pending submissions first, then a requested finalize, as `{ kind: 'finalize', request: <the request id, a string>, model: cfg.agents.models.finalizer, next }`.
    - When it hands out a finalize, it calls `rt.listeners.setBusy(windowId, true)` and `changed(ref)`, so the page shows "Claude is writing the final." live.
    - Task 6 makes `finished.submission` optional and adds `finished.finalize: string` (the request id).
    - A window that comes back while its finalize is still `writing` gets `finishFinalize`, whether or not it passed `finished.finalize`.
    - Task 8 adds a pending detect for the project's repo, handed out after the finalize, as `{ kind: 'detect-profile', repo, clone, model: cfg.agents.models.repoSetup, next }`, and adds `finished.detect: string` (the repo).
  - Requeueing also calls `requeueFinalize`.
  - `/finalize` takes `{ repo, project, request, markdown }` and calls `saveProposal` with `name: finalName(project.source.path)`.
  - `/context` also accepts `{ finalize: true }` and calls `finalizePack`. The service reads the rules from `<configDir>/outputs/finalize.md` (defaults if that file is missing).
  - `/open` calls `rt.withLock(key, () => recordClone(ref.dir, git.root, ctx.home))` for every open, by plan or by project id (Task 6).
  - Task 8: when a detect is pending for the matched repo and handed to no window yet, `/open` returns `{ kind: 'needs-profile', redetect: true, name, remote, clone, suggestedName: name, model, next }` before opening.
  - Task 8: `/repo-profile` with no profile in the body returns `{ kind: 'redetect', profile, remote, clone }` while a detect is pending, so the repo-setup agent sees the current profile. A save with a pending detect stores `mergeDetected(existing, detected)` instead of refusing, then calls `finishDetect`. A first profile save also calls `finishDetect`, so "Last detected" covers first detections.
- **Runtime (Task 8):** `Runtime.detects: Map<repo, { windowId, at, back }>`, and `Runtime.now`, the listeners' clock.
  - A detect request goes to one window. A window that isn't back holds it while it's alive, or for 10 minutes after `at`, whichever lasts longer, so a window that got it through `/open` keeps it while its repo-setup runs.
  - Once that window comes back, it isn't handed out again until **Detect again** is pressed again, so a repo-setup subagent that can't save never runs in a loop.
  - Pressing **Detect again** again, even while a request is pending, clears the hand-out, so the next window gets the request afresh.
- **Config routes:**
  - `POST /api/repos/:name/detect` calls `requestDetect` and returns `{ ok: true }`.
  - `GET /api/config` gains `detect: { pending: string[]; last: Record<string, string> }`.

### MCP and plugin (Task 7)

- **`dp_finalize`:** `{ repo, project, request, markdown }`, forwarded to `/api/claude/finalize`.
- **`dp_context`** gains `finalize?: boolean`.
- **`dp_wait`'s description** mentions the `finalize` and `detect-profile` kinds and `finished.finalize` / `finished.detect`. Task 7 adds both, so Task 8 changes only `dp_repo_profile`'s description.
- **SKILL.md** is replaced whole in Task 7, then whole again in Task 8: Task 7's text plus the redetect and `detect-profile` parts. No other task touches it.
- **`plugin/agents/finalizer.md`:**
  - tools: `Read, Grep, Glob, mcp__plugin_dev-plumbing_dp__dp_context, mcp__plugin_dev-plumbing_dp__dp_finalize`;
  - it follows the pack's `rules` and uses only the pack's tokens for drawings;
  - parked items aren't in the pack, and it never writes them into the final;
  - it calls `dp_finalize` once, and retries at most three times on refusal;
  - it replies with one line.

### Web (Tasks 9–11)

```ts
// web/src/api/client.ts
api.finalize(repo, id): Promise<FinalizeView>
api.startFinalize(repo, id): Promise<StartFinalizeResponse>    // { request: FinalizeRequest; listening: ListeningState; message: string }
api.acceptFinal(repo, id, clone: string): Promise<AcceptFinalResponse>   // { exportedTo: NonNullable<PlumbingProject['docs']['exportedTo']>; nextCommand: string }
api.discardProposal(repo, id): Promise<{ ok: true }>
api.detectRepo(name: string): Promise<{ ok: true }>
// router: /p/$repo/$project/finalize → FinalizePage
```

- The header's **Finalize spec** links to the Finalize page.
  - It's disabled when `!home.finalize.canStart && home.finalize.state === null`, with the title `blockedReason(blockingCount)`: "1 item blocks Finalize" / "N items block Finalize" (`blockedReason` is exported from `ProjectHeader.tsx`).
  - It reads **Finalize again** when the project is Finalized.
  - On phones, it's in a `md:hidden` row of `ProjectHeader`, with the reason beside it, never in `ProjectNav`, because `project-home.spec.ts` matches the nav text "Final".
- **The request id:** the web reads it from `GET …/finalize` (`view.request.id`). The e2e tests that act as Claude read `/wait`'s `request` as the id string.
- **e2e isolation:**
  - The finalize specs use their own projects, because `/wait` hands out any leftover requested finalize.
  - `settings-detect.spec.ts` clears its own detect request in the same test, because a pending detect turns every `/open` for that repo into `needs-profile`.
- Test ids:
  - `finalize-checklist`, `checklist-blocking`, `checklist-defaults`, `checklist-parked`, `checklist-unreviewed`;
  - `finalize-status`, `proposal-preview`, `proposal-diff`, `accept-form`;
  - `final-done`, `next-command`, `detect-repo`;
  - added by the tasks: `finalize` (the page), `proposal`, `mermaid-block`, `accept-target`, `final-exported`, `repo-profile`.

---

### Task 1: The finalize checklist, and the clones a project was opened from

Before you start Finalize, the app needs to know what stops it, what will use a default, what's parked and what nobody looked at. This task adds that checklist to core, gives the project home what the header's **Finalize spec** button needs, and makes a project remember every clone it was opened from, so Accept (Task 4) can offer them. It also adds `readFinalize`, so the project home can show how far a finalize got; Task 3 adds the rest of the request lifecycle to the same file.

**Files:**
- Create:
  - `packages/core/src/schemas/finalize.ts`
  - `packages/core/src/store/checklist.ts`
  - `packages/core/src/store/finalize.ts` (only `readFinalize` for now; Task 3 fills it in)
  - `packages/core/test/checklist.test.ts`
- Modify:
  - `packages/core/src/schemas/index.ts` (export `./finalize`)
  - `packages/core/src/schemas/project.ts` (`clones`, `exportedTo.assets`)
  - `packages/core/src/schemas/views.ts` (`ProjectHome.finalize`)
  - `packages/core/src/store/io.ts` (`projectFiles(dir).finalize`)
  - `packages/core/src/store/open.ts` (`openPlan` records the clone; new `recordClone`)
  - `packages/core/src/store/projects.ts` (`loadProjectHome` fills `finalize`)
  - `packages/core/src/index.ts` (export `./store/checklist` and `./store/finalize`)
  - `packages/core/test/fixtures.ts` (`seedProject` writes `clones`)
- Test:
  - `packages/core/test/checklist.test.ts`
  - `packages/core/test/open.test.ts`
  - `packages/core/test/projects.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Plans 1–3):
  - `displayStatus`, `changeState`, `Item`, `Thread`, `HistoryEntry`, `PlumbingType`, `ProjectHome`, `plumbingProjectSchema` (`@dev-plumbing/core/schemas`);
  - `latestOpen` (`store/threads.ts`): the options on Claude's last message, or null when that message resolved the thread, or the thread is with Claude or resolved. Its `message` is the Claude message, so `message.opening` says the importer wrote it;
  - `readItems`, `readThreads`, `readHistory`, `readJsonFile`, `readProjectFile`, `writeProjectFile`, `projectFiles` (`store/io.ts`); `tildify` (`store/open.ts`); `expandHome` (`paths.ts`).
- Produces, as in the header's Contracts (`checklistFrom`, `changesSinceFinal`, `recordClone` and `projectFiles(dir).finalize` are additions that later tasks use):
  - `@dev-plumbing/core/schemas` (`schemas/finalize.ts`): `finalizeStateValues`, `FinalizeState`, `FinalizeRequest` (without `requeuedAt` and `proposal.assets`, which Task 3 adds), `finalizeRequestSchema: z.ZodType<FinalizeRequest>`, `ChecklistEntry`, `FinalizeChecklist`.
  - `plumbingProjectSchema` gains `clones: z.array(z.string()).default([])`, and `docs.exportedTo` gains `assets: z.array(z.string()).default([])`. A `project.json` without them still loads.
  - `ProjectHome.finalize: { canStart: boolean; blockingCount: number; state: FinalizeState | null; changesSinceFinal: number }`.
  - `@dev-plumbing/core`:
    - `finalizeChecklist(dir: string, types: PlumbingType[]): Promise<FinalizeChecklist>` (`store/checklist.ts`);
    - `checklistFrom(o: { items: Item[]; threads: Thread[]; history: HistoryEntry[]; types: PlumbingType[] }): FinalizeChecklist`, the same from data already read (the project home uses it, and so can Task 6);
    - `changesSinceFinal(history: HistoryEntry[], exportedAt: string | undefined): number`: history entries applied, and not undone, after `exportedAt`; 0 without a final (Task 6's `FinalizeView.changesSinceFinal` uses it too);
    - `recordClone(dir: string, clone: string, home?: string): Promise<string[]>` (`store/open.ts`). Task 6's `/open` calls it for every open, under the project lock;
    - `readFinalize(dir: string): Promise<FinalizeRequest | null>` (`store/finalize.ts`). Task 3 imports it from the same file;
    - `projectFiles(dir).finalize` is `<dir>/finalize.json`.
- **Checklist rules** (spec §10.5, §11's gate). Items are listed by plumbing type `order`, then title, and each item appears in at most one list:
  - **Parked** threads go in `parked` with "Parked." and never block.
  - **Blocking**, with the first reason that applies:
    1. the thread is with Claude: "Claude is working on it.";
    2. `fields.blocking === 'true'` and not resolved: "Blocking question, not resolved.";
    3. `fields.severity` is critical or high (any case, any plumbing type: read the field, never the type id) and not resolved: "High-severity concern, not resolved.";
    4. Claude's latest open options carry a `change`, and that message isn't the importer's opening one: "A proposal is waiting for your answer.";
    5. a history entry for the thread is a pending small edit (`kind: 'small-edit'`, `changeState === 'pending'`): "A small edit is waiting to be applied.". This blocks even on a resolved thread.
  - Otherwise, resolved threads are on no list.
  - **Defaults:** an unresolved item with a non-empty `fields.default` goes in `defaults` with "No answer yet; the default will be used." and `defaultValue` (trimmed).
  - **Unreviewed:** any other unresolved item whose thread has no `you` message goes in `unreviewed` with "Nobody has answered here.".
  - Items of disabled plumbing types, and items whose thread is missing, are left out. An item whose type is unknown uses its type id as `typeTitle`.
  - `canStart` is `blocking.length === 0`.
- **Clones:** `openPlan` writes `clones: [<the clone, tildified>]` when it creates a project. `recordClone` reads `project.json`, builds `[source.clone, ...clones, clone]` with every entry put through `tildify(expandHome(p, home), home)`, drops repeats, and writes only when the list changed. So the source clone is always first, `~/Work/acme/` and `~/Work/acme` are one clone, and an old project gets its source clone back. `openPlan`'s reopen branch doesn't write, because it runs under the folder lock, not the project lock.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing checklist tests**

`packages/core/test/checklist.test.ts`:
```ts
import { afterAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { HistoryEntry, Message, Option } from '../src/schemas';
import { changesSinceFinal, checklistFrom, finalizeChecklist } from '../src/store/checklist';
import { writeHistoryEntry } from '../src/store/io';
import { removeTempDirs } from '../../../testkit/tmp';
import { listType, pair, seedProject, TYPES } from './fixtures';

afterAll(removeTempDirs);

const AT = '2026-10-01T09:00:00.000Z';
const WITH_CHANGE: Option[] = [
  { id: 'email', label: 'Email only', change: { md: [{ find: 'sends a reminder', replace: 'sends an email reminder' }] } },
  { id: 'sms', label: 'SMS too' },
];
const claude = (id: string, extra: Partial<Extract<Message, { author: 'claude' }>> = {}): Message => ({ id, at: AT, author: 'claude', text: 'Which one?', ...extra });
const you = (id: string): Message => ({ id, at: AT, author: 'you', optionId: 'custom', text: 'Email only, please.' });

const edit = (id: string, threadId: string, extra: Partial<HistoryEntry> = {}): HistoryEntry => ({
  id,
  at: AT,
  threadId,
  kind: 'small-edit',
  summary: 'Say UTC',
  change: { md: [{ find: 'daily job', replace: 'daily job (UTC)' }] },
  itemsBefore: {},
  itemsAfter: {},
  ...extra,
});

/** One item for every place on the checklist, and some that belong nowhere. */
function everyKind() {
  return [
    // Reviewed and still open, with nothing that blocks: on no list.
    pair('a-reviewed', { type: 'architecture', title: 'Daily job', messages: [claude('m1'), you('y1')] }),
    pair('q-block', { title: 'Who gets reminders at launch?', fields: { blocking: 'true' } }),
    pair('q-block-done', { title: 'Which channels?', fields: { blocking: 'true' }, status: 'resolved' }),
    pair('q-claude', { title: 'How often?', status: 'with_claude' }),
    pair('q-proposal', { title: 'Reminder copy', messages: [claude('m1', { opening: true }), you('y1'), claude('m2', { options: WITH_CHANGE })] }),
    // The importer's opening options aren't a proposal, so this one just uses its default.
    pair('q-opening', { title: 'Lead time', fields: { default: '5 days' }, options: WITH_CHANGE }),
    pair('q-edit', { title: 'Time zone', status: 'resolved' }),
    pair('q-default', { title: 'Sender address', fields: { default: '  reminders@acme.test ' } }),
    pair('q-parked', { title: 'SMS later?', fields: { blocking: 'true' }, status: 'parked' }),
    pair('c-high', { type: 'concerns', title: 'Duplicate emails', fields: { severity: 'high' }, messages: [claude('m1'), you('y1')] }),
    pair('c-crit', { type: 'concerns', title: 'Unsubscribe link', fields: { severity: 'Critical' } }),
    pair('c-low', { type: 'concerns', title: 'Slow query', fields: { severity: 'low' } }),
  ];
}

const row = (id: string, title: string, typeTitle: string, reason: string) => ({ itemId: id, threadId: `t-${id}`, title, typeTitle, reason });

describe('the finalize checklist', () => {
  it('lists what blocks, what uses a default, what is parked and what nobody reviewed', async () => {
    const dir = await seedProject({ pairs: everyKind() });
    await writeHistoryEntry(dir, edit('c-1', 't-q-edit'));
    await writeHistoryEntry(dir, edit('c-2', 't-a-reviewed', { appliedAt: AT }));
    expect(await finalizeChecklist(dir, TYPES)).toEqual({
      blocking: [
        row('q-claude', 'How often?', 'Questions', 'Claude is working on it.'),
        row('q-proposal', 'Reminder copy', 'Questions', 'A proposal is waiting for your answer.'),
        row('q-edit', 'Time zone', 'Questions', 'A small edit is waiting to be applied.'),
        row('q-block', 'Who gets reminders at launch?', 'Questions', 'Blocking question, not resolved.'),
        row('c-high', 'Duplicate emails', 'Concerns', 'High-severity concern, not resolved.'),
        row('c-crit', 'Unsubscribe link', 'Concerns', 'High-severity concern, not resolved.'),
      ],
      defaults: [
        { ...row('q-opening', 'Lead time', 'Questions', 'No answer yet; the default will be used.'), defaultValue: '5 days' },
        { ...row('q-default', 'Sender address', 'Questions', 'No answer yet; the default will be used.'), defaultValue: 'reminders@acme.test' },
      ],
      parked: [row('q-parked', 'SMS later?', 'Questions', 'Parked.')],
      unreviewed: [row('c-low', 'Slow query', 'Concerns', 'Nobody has answered here.')],
      canStart: false,
    });
  });

  it('can start once nothing blocks', () => {
    const pairs = [
      pair('q1', { title: 'Who gets reminders?', fields: { blocking: 'true' }, status: 'resolved' }),
      pair('c1', { type: 'concerns', title: 'Duplicate emails', fields: { severity: 'high' }, status: 'parked' }),
      pair('q2', { title: 'Lead time', fields: { default: '5 days' } }),
    ];
    const list = checklistFrom({ items: pairs.map((p) => p.item), threads: pairs.map((p) => p.thread), history: [], types: TYPES });
    expect(list.canStart).toBe(true);
    expect(list.blocking).toEqual([]);
    expect(list.parked.map((e) => e.itemId)).toEqual(['c1']);
    expect(list.defaults.map((e) => e.itemId)).toEqual(['q2']);
  });

  it('reads severity from the field, whatever the type is called, and skips disabled types', () => {
    const risks = listType('risks', { title: 'Risks', order: 7, fields: ['severity'] });
    const hidden = listType('hidden', { title: 'Hidden', order: 8, enabled: false });
    const pairs = [
      pair('r1', { type: 'risks', title: 'Data loss', fields: { severity: 'HIGH ' } }),
      pair('h1', { type: 'hidden', title: 'Old question', fields: { blocking: 'true' } }),
    ];
    const list = checklistFrom({ items: pairs.map((p) => p.item), threads: pairs.map((p) => p.thread), history: [], types: [...TYPES, risks, hidden] });
    expect(list.blocking).toEqual([row('r1', 'Data loss', 'Risks', 'High-severity concern, not resolved.')]);
    expect([...list.defaults, ...list.parked, ...list.unreviewed]).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('counts the changes applied since the last final', () => {
    const final = '2026-10-02T10:00:00.000Z';
    const history = [
      edit('c-1', 't-q1', { appliedAt: '2026-10-02T09:00:00.000Z' }),
      edit('c-2', 't-q1', { appliedAt: '2026-10-02T11:00:00.000Z' }),
      edit('c-3', 't-q1', { kind: 'accept', appliedAt: '2026-10-02T12:00:00.000Z' }),
      edit('c-4', 't-q1', { appliedAt: '2026-10-02T11:00:00.000Z', undoneAt: '2026-10-02T11:30:00.000Z' }),
      edit('c-5', 't-q1'),
    ];
    expect(changesSinceFinal(history, final)).toBe(2);
    expect(changesSinceFinal(history, undefined)).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

    expect(await fs.readFile(file, 'utf8')).toBe(compact);
  });

  it('reads a project.json from before clones and asset lists, and puts the source clone first', async () => {
    const at = '2026-10-02T10:00:00.000Z';
    const dir = await seedProject();
    const file = path.join(dir, 'project.json');
    const old = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(file, 'utf8'));
    delete old.clones;
    old.docs = { ...old.docs, final: 'docs/final.md', exportedTo: { clone: '/tmp/acme', path: 'docs/specs/restock.final.md', at } };
    await fs.writeFile(file, JSON.stringify(old));
    const project = await readProjectFile(dir);
    expect(project.clones).toEqual([]);
    expect(project.docs.exportedTo).toEqual({ clone: '/tmp/acme', path: 'docs/specs/restock.final.md', at, assets: [] });
    expect(await recordClone(dir, '/tmp/acme-2', home)).toEqual(['/tmp/acme', '/tmp/acme-2']);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';

export const finalizeStateValues = ['requested', 'writing', 'proposed', 'failed'] as const;
export type FinalizeState = (typeof finalizeStateValues)[number];

/** finalize.json in the project folder. Absent when no finalize is under way. */
export type FinalizeRequest = {
  id: string;
  state: FinalizeState;
  requestedAt: string;
  /** The listening window that took it, through dp_wait. */
  pickedUpAt?: string;
  pickedUpBy?: string;
  proposal?: { at: string; draftHash: string; file: 'docs/final.proposed.md'; length: number };
  failedAt?: string;
  reason?: string;
};

export const finalizeRequestSchema: z.ZodType<FinalizeRequest> = z.object({
  id: z.string().min(1),
  state: z.enum(finalizeStateValues),
  requestedAt: z.string(),
  pickedUpAt: z.string().optional(),
  pickedUpBy: z.string().optional(),
  proposal: z
    .object({ at: z.string(), draftHash: z.string(), file: z.literal('docs/final.proposed.md'), length: z.number().int().min(0) })
    .optional(),
  failedAt: z.string().optional(),
  reason: z.string().optional(),
});

/** One line on the Finalize page's checklist, linking to the item's thread. */
export type ChecklistEntry = { itemId: string; threadId: string; title: string; typeTitle: string; reason: string };

/** What the Finalize page lists before you start. Each item appears at most once. */
export type FinalizeChecklist = {
  /** "These block Finalize" */
  blocking: ChecklistEntry[];
  /** "These will use their default" */
  defaults: (ChecklistEntry & { defaultValue: string })[];
  /** "Parked: left out of the final" */
  parked: ChecklistEntry[];
  /** "Nobody has reviewed these": a warning, never a block. */
  unreviewed: ChecklistEntry[];
  /** blocking.length === 0 */
  canStart: boolean;
};
```

```ts
import { finalizeRequestSchema, type FinalizeRequest } from '../schemas';
import { projectFiles, readJsonFile } from './io';

// Task 3 adds the rest of the finalize request lifecycle to this file.

/**
 * finalize.json, or null when no finalize is under way. A damaged file reads as none: it only ever holds the one
 * request, so Start finalize can replace it.
 */
export async function readFinalize(dir: string): Promise<FinalizeRequest | null> {
  const r = await readJsonFile(projectFiles(dir).finalize);
  if (!r.ok) return null;
  const parsed = finalizeRequestSchema.safeParse(r.value);
  return parsed.success ? parsed.data : null;
}
```

`packages/core/src/store/checklist.ts`:
```ts
import {
  changeState,
  displayStatus,
  type ChecklistEntry,
  type DisplayStatus,
  type FinalizeChecklist,
  type HistoryEntry,
  type Item,
  type PlumbingType,
  type Thread,
} from '../schemas';
import { readHistory, readItems, readThreads } from './io';
import { latestOpen } from './threads';

/** Concern severities that block Finalize until they're resolved. Read from the field, whatever the type. */
const HIGH_SEVERITY = new Set(['critical', 'high']);

/** Why this item's thread blocks Finalize, or null. The first reason that applies wins. */
function blockingReason(item: Item, thread: Thread, status: DisplayStatus, pendingEdit: boolean): string | null {
  if (status === 'with_claude') return 'Claude is working on it.';
  const unresolved = status !== 'resolved';
  if (unresolved && item.fields?.blocking === 'true') return 'Blocking question, not resolved.';
  if (unresolved && HIGH_SEVERITY.has(item.fields?.severity?.trim().toLowerCase() ?? '')) return 'High-severity concern, not resolved.';
  // A proposal is a reply that offers a change. The importer's opening options are the item's first choices, not a proposal.
  const open = latestOpen(thread);
  if (open && !open.message.opening && open.options.some((o) => o.change)) return 'A proposal is waiting for your answer.';
  if (pendingEdit) return 'A small edit is waiting to be applied.';
  return null;
}

/**
 * The Finalize checklist from items, threads and history already read. Items are listed by plumbing type order,
 * then title, and each appears at most once: parked, else blocking, else (unless resolved) on a default, else
 * unreviewed when nobody has answered. Items of disabled types are left out, as the app doesn't show them.
 */
export function checklistFrom(o: { items: Item[]; threads: Thread[]; history: HistoryEntry[]; types: PlumbingType[] }): FinalizeChecklist {
  const threadById = new Map(o.threads.map((t) => [t.id, t]));
  const typeById = new Map(o.types.map((t) => [t.id, t]));
  const pendingEdits = new Set(o.history.filter((h) => h.kind === 'small-edit' && changeState(h) === 'pending').map((h) => h.threadId));
  const order = (i: Item) => typeById.get(i.type)?.order ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
  const items = o.items.filter((i) => typeById.get(i.type)?.enabled !== false).sort((a, b) => order(a) - order(b) || a.title.localeCompare(b.title));
  const list: FinalizeChecklist = { blocking: [], defaults: [], parked: [], unreviewed: [], canStart: true };
  for (const item of items) {
    const thread = threadById.get(item.threadId);
    if (!thread) continue;
    const entry = (reason: string): ChecklistEntry => ({
      itemId: item.id,
      threadId: thread.id,
      title: item.title,
      typeTitle: typeById.get(item.type)?.title ?? item.type,
      reason,
    });
    const status = displayStatus(thread);
    if (status === 'parked') {
      list.parked.push(entry('Parked.'));
      continue;
    }
    const blocked = blockingReason(item, thread, status, pendingEdits.has(thread.id));
    if (blocked) {
      list.blocking.push(entry(blocked));
      continue;
    }
    if (status === 'resolved') continue;
    const defaultValue = item.fields?.default?.trim();
    if (defaultValue) list.defaults.push({ ...entry('No answer yet; the default will be used.'), defaultValue });
    else if (!thread.messages.some((m) => m.author === 'you')) list.unreviewed.push(entry('Nobody has answered here.'));
  }
  list.canStart = list.blocking.length === 0;
  return list;
}

/** What blocks Finalize, what will use a default, what's parked and what nobody reviewed (spec §10.5, §11). */
export async function finalizeChecklist(dir: string, types: PlumbingType[]): Promise<FinalizeChecklist> {
  const [{ values: items }, { values: threads }, history] = await Promise.all([readItems(dir), readThreads(dir), readHistory(dir)]);
  return checklistFrom({ items, threads, history, types });
}

/** Changes applied, and not undone, after the last final was accepted at `exportedAt`. 0 when there's no final. */
export function changesSinceFinal(history: HistoryEntry[], exportedAt: string | undefined): number {
  if (!exportedAt) return 0;
  return history.filter((h) => changeState(h) === 'applied' && (h.appliedAt ?? '') > exportedAt).length;
}
```

- Add `recordClone` just above `linkIntoClone`'s doc comment (`/**` then ` * Spec §6.3: a link named linkName…`):
```ts
/**
 * Remembers a clone the project was opened from, so Accept can offer it. Each clone once, ~-shortened, the source
 * clone first. Writes nothing when the clone is already known. The service calls it for every open (a new plan, the
 * same plan from another clone, or a project picked by id), under the project's lock. Returns the clones.
 */
export async function recordClone(dir: string, clone: string, home?: string): Promise<string[]> {
  const project = await readProjectFile(dir);
  const short = (p: string) => tildify(expandHome(p, home), home);
  const clones = [...new Set([project.source.clone, ...project.clones, clone].map(short))];
  if (clones.length === project.clones.length && clones.every((c, i) => c === project.clones[i])) return clones;
  await writeProjectFile(dir, { ...project, clones });
  return clones;
}
```

### Task 2: Mermaid, steps, schema blocks, assets and tokens

The final's drawings come from the data you agreed, never from the finalizer's hand (Decision 2). This task adds `core/src/finalExport.ts`: pure functions that turn a diagram into a Mermaid flowchart, a flow into a Mermaid sequence diagram or numbered steps, a table into its exact schema diff and migration notes, and a mockup into a plain HTML file. `expandTokens` replaces each `{{kind:itemId}}` token in the finalizer's Markdown with the right block, and refuses the whole document, listing every problem, when a token is unknown or doesn't fit its item. Task 3 calls it when the finalizer sends a final, Task 4 writes the mockup files, and Task 5 lists the tokens in the finalizer's pack.

**Files:**
- Create:
  - `packages/core/src/finalExport.ts`
  - `packages/core/src/finalExport.test.ts`
- Modify:
  - `packages/core/src/index.ts` (export `./finalExport`; it isn't exported from `schemas`)
- Test:
  - `packages/core/src/finalExport.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Plan 3, `@dev-plumbing/core/schemas`): `parseData`, `dataKindOf`, `DataKind`, `DiagramData`, `FlowData`, `TableDiff`, `Item`, `PlumbingType`. Nothing from Task 1.
- Produces (exported from `@dev-plumbing/core`), exactly as in the header's Contracts:
  - `diagramMermaid(d: DiagramData): string`
  - `sequenceMermaid(f: FlowData): string`
  - `userFlowSteps(f: FlowData): string`
  - `schemaBlock(t: TableDiff): string`
  - `migrationList(t: TableDiff): string`
  - `mockupAssetName(itemId: string, side: 'after' | 'before'): string`, which is `${itemId}.${side}.html`
  - `mockupAssetHtml(o: { title: string; app: string; route?: string; kitFiles: string[]; body: string }): string`
  - `TOKEN_PATTERN: RegExp`, exactly `/\{\{(diagram|sequence|steps|schema|migration|mockup):([a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*)(?::(after|before))?\}\}/g`
  - `TokenContext = { items: Item[]; types: PlumbingType[]; assetsDir: string }`, where `assetsDir` is the relative folder name, `<name>.assets`
  - `expandTokens(markdown, ctx)`: `{ ok: true; markdown: string; assets: { itemId: string; side: 'after' | 'before' }[] } | { ok: false; problems: string[] }`
  - `availableTokens(items: Item[], types: PlumbingType[]): string[]`
- **What each block looks like:**
  - **Flowchart:** a ` ```mermaid ` fence with `flowchart LR`; one `subgraph g_<id>["label"]` … `end` per group that has boxes; boxes as `n_<id>["label"]`; lines as `-->` or, for `style: 'dashed'`, `-.->`, with `|"label"|` when the line has a label; then the four `classDef` lines (`new stroke:#5f8a5b`, `changed stroke:#c07a2c`, `unchanged stroke:#cbcbcb`, `external stroke-dasharray:4 3`) and one `class n_<id> <status>` line per box.
  - **Sequence diagram:** `sequenceDiagram`, `autonumber`, then `participant l_<id> as <label>` per lane. Steps go in `n` order:
    - a step with `from` and `to` is `l_a->>l_b: label` (a self-call when they're the same lane);
    - a step with only one of them is `Note over l_x: <n>. label`;
    - a step with neither is `Note over <first lane>,<last lane>: <n>. label`.
    
    When an arrow's `n` isn't the next number `autonumber` would give it (a note came before it, or the numbers skip), an `autonumber <n>` line restarts the count, so the arrows' numbers always match the steps. A step's `systemNote` is `Note right of <the lane it ends on>: note`.
  - **User-flow steps:** `1. label`, with `   - System: note` under a step that has a `systemNote`. The indent is the width of the step's marker, so it stays under step 10 too.
  - **Schema diff:** the `schemaDiff` text exactly, trailing newlines trimmed, in a ` ```diff ` fence. The fence is longer than any run of backticks inside it.
  - **Migration notes:** `- **<label>:** text`, one per entry, in order, with the Database screen's labels: Additive, Backfill, Destructive, Data risk, Rollback.
  - **Mockup link:** `[After mockup](<assetsDir>/<itemId>.after.html)` (or `Before`). The path is percent-encoded, with `(` and `)` as `%28` and `%29`, so a plan named `restock (v2).md` still links.
  - **Mockup file:** `<!doctype html>`, then a comment naming the app, route and kit files, then `<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>` with the title HTML-escaped, then the body markup as it is. There's no script and no style.
- **Escaping:**
  - Labels, notes and participant names are put on one line (whitespace runs, newlines included, become a space).
  - These characters become Mermaid entity codes: `"` → `#quot;`, `` ` `` → `#96;`, `;` → `#59;`, `%` → `#37;`, `<` → `#lt;`, `>` → `#gt;`.
  - Ids are prefixed (`n_`, `g_`, `l_`), and every character but letters, digits and `_` becomes `_`. Two ids that come out the same get `_2`, `_3`.
  - A mockup file's comment never contains `--`.
- **Facts checked for this task**, by parsing every golden string and 1,200 generated diagrams with `mermaid.parse` from Mermaid 12.1.0 (under jsdom, outside the repo; this adds no dependency):
  - In a quoted label, `#quot;` is read as a quote, and brackets, braces, pipes and `-->` are plain text.
  - A sequence diagram keeps quotes around a participant's alias literally (`as "Web app"` shows the quotes), so aliases aren't quoted. They are escaped instead.
  - `;` ends a statement in a sequence diagram, even inside a message: `a; l_a->>l_a: injected` adds a second message.
  - `%%{` inside a quoted flowchart label is a parse error.
  - A quoted label that starts with a backtick is read as a Markdown string, and fails to parse.
  - `autonumber 4` mid-diagram restarts the numbering at 4.
  - `n_account-page` parses, but an id containing `--` doesn't, so ids keep only letters, digits and `_`.
- **Token rules.** Each problem is `<token>: <why>`, once per distinct token, in the order the tokens appear, followed by any leftovers:
  - The item must exist: `there's no item "<id>".`
  - Only mockup tokens take a side: `only mockup tokens take :after or :before.`
  - The item's plumbing type must draw the right kind of data (from `dataKindOf`): diagram for `diagram`; flows for `sequence` and `steps`; database for `schema` and `migration`; mockups for `mockup`. Otherwise: `"<title>" isn't a diagram item.` / `a flow item` / `a database item` / `a UI item`.
  - The data must parse (`parseData`): `"<title>" has no diagram data.` / `flow data` / `table data` / `mockup data`.
  - `sequence` needs a system or both flow: `that flow is a user flow; use {{steps:<id>}}.`
  - `steps` needs a user or both flow: `that flow is a system flow; use {{sequence:<id>}}.`
  - `schema` needs a non-empty `schemaDiff`: `"<title>" has no schema diff.`
  - `migration` needs at least one entry: `"<title>" has no migration notes.`
  - `mockup` needs a side: `say which side: {{mockup:<id>:after}} or {{mockup:<id>:before}}.`
  - `mockup` needs markup on that side: `"<title>" has no before mockup.` (or `after`).
  - Every token but `mockup` becomes a block, so it must be alone on its line (leading whitespace is fine, and the block is indented to match, so a token inside a list item stays in the list): `put this token on a line of its own.`
  - Any `{{` left in the finalizer's own text (outside the tokens that matched) is `Unknown token: {{…}}.`, showing up to the closing `}}` on the same line, or the next 20 characters.
  - `assets` lists each mockup item and side once, in the order first linked.
  - `expandTokens` makes its own copy of `TOKEN_PATTERN`, so the shared regex's `lastIndex` never matters.
- **`availableTokens`:** one line per token that would expand, for items whose id matches the token pattern, ordered by plumbing type `order`, then title:
  - `{{diagram:<id>}}: the Mermaid flowchart of "<title>".`
  - `{{sequence:<id>}}: the Mermaid sequence diagram of "<title>".` (system and both flows)
  - `{{steps:<id>}}: the numbered steps of "<title>".` (user and both flows)
  - `{{schema:<id>}}: the schema diff of "<title>".` and `{{migration:<id>}}: the migration notes of "<title>".` (when each exists)
  - `{{mockup:<id>:<side>}}: a link to the <side> mockup of "<title>", copied into the assets folder.` (per side with markup)

`packages/core/src/finalExport.test.ts`:
````ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  availableTokens,
  diagramMermaid,
  expandTokens,
  migrationList,
  mockupAssetHtml,
  mockupAssetName,
  schemaBlock,
  sequenceMermaid,
  TOKEN_PATTERN,
  userFlowSteps,
  type TokenContext,
} from './finalExport';
import type { DiagramData, FlowData, Item, MockupData, PlumbingType, TableDiff } from './schemas';

const type = (id: string, title: string, screen: PlumbingType['screen'], order: number): PlumbingType => ({
  id,
  title,
  order,
  screen,

describe('tokens', () => {
  it('uses the pattern from the plan', () => {
    expect(TOKEN_PATTERN.source).toBe('\\{\\{(diagram|sequence|steps|schema|migration|mockup):([a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*)(?::(after|before))?\\}\\}');
    expect(TOKEN_PATTERN.flags).toBe('g');
  });

  it('expands every kind of token, keeping list indentation', () => {
    const markdown = [
      '## Architecture',
      '',
      '{{diagram:architecture-system}}',
      '',
      '## Data model',
      '',
      '{{schema:database-restock}}',
      '',
      '{{migration:database-restock}}',
      '',
      '## UI changes',
      '',
      'Account page: {{mockup:ui-account:after}}, and today {{mockup:ui-account:before}}.',
      '',
      '## Flows',
      '',
      '- Checkout:',
      '  {{steps:flows-checkout}}',
      '- Nightly sync:',
      '',
      '  {{sequence:flows-sync}}',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const r = expandTokens(markdown, ctx);
    expect(r.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!r.ok) return;
    expect(r.markdown).toBe(
      [
        '## Architecture',
        '',
        diagramMermaid(DIAGRAM),
        '',
        '## Data model',
        '',
        schemaBlock(TABLE),
        '',
        migrationList(TABLE),
        '',
        '## UI changes',
        '',
        'Account page: [After mockup](restock.assets/ui-account.after.html), and today [Before mockup](restock.assets/ui-account.before.html).',
        '',
        '## Flows',
        '',
        '- Checkout:',
        '  1. Opens checkout',
        '  2. Pays',
        '     - System: Charges the card.',
        '- Nightly sync:',
        '',
        '  ```mermaid',
        '  sequenceDiagram',
        '    autonumber',
        '    participant l_job as Nightly job',
        '    l_job->>l_job: Syncs stock',
        '  ```',
        '',
      ].join('\n'),
    );
    expect(r.assets).toEqual([
      { itemId: 'ui-account', side: 'after' },
      { itemId: 'ui-account', side: 'before' },
    ]);
    expect(r.markdown).not.toContain('{{');
  });

  it('collects each mockup asset once, and links a folder name with spaces safely', () => {
    const r = expandTokens('{{mockup:ui-banner:after}} and again {{mockup:ui-banner:after}}, then {{mockup:ui-account:after}}', { ...ctx, assetsDir: 'restock (v2).assets' });
    expect(r).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      markdown:
        '[After mockup](restock%20%28v2%29.assets/ui-banner.after.html) and again [After mockup](restock%20%28v2%29.assets/ui-banner.after.html), then [After mockup](restock%20%28v2%29.assets/ui-account.after.html)',
      assets: [
        { itemId: 'ui-banner', side: 'after' },
        { itemId: 'ui-account', side: 'after' },
      ],
    });
  });

  it('unknown or mismatched tokens are refused', () => {
    const markdown = [
      '{{diagram:architecture-gone}}',
      '{{diagram:questions-who}}',
      '{{diagram:architecture-old}}',
      '{{diagram:architecture-system:after}}',
      '{{sequence:flows-checkout}}',
      '{{steps:flows-sync}}',
      '{{schema:database-customer}}',
      '{{migration:database-customer}}',
      '{{schema:ui-account}}',
      'See {{mockup:ui-banner:before}} and {{mockup:ui-banner}}.',
      'Inline {{diagram:architecture-system}} is not a block.',
      '{{diagram:architecture-gone}}',
      '{{nonsense}} and {{ diagram:architecture-system }} and {{Diagram:architecture-system}}',
      'An open {{ that never closes',
    ].join('\n');
    expect(expandTokens(markdown, ctx)).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      problems: [
        '{{diagram:architecture-gone}}: there\'s no item "architecture-gone".',
        '{{diagram:questions-who}}: "Who gets reminders?" isn\'t a diagram item.',
        '{{diagram:architecture-old}}: "Old diagram" has no diagram data.',
        '{{diagram:architecture-system:after}}: only mockup tokens take :after or :before.',
        '{{sequence:flows-checkout}}: that flow is a user flow; use {{steps:flows-checkout}}.',
        '{{steps:flows-sync}}: that flow is a system flow; use {{sequence:flows-sync}}.',
        '{{schema:database-customer}}: "Customer table" has no schema diff.',
        '{{migration:database-customer}}: "Customer table" has no migration notes.',
        '{{schema:ui-account}}: "Account page" isn\'t a database item.',
        '{{mockup:ui-banner:before}}: "Reminder banner" has no before mockup.',
        '{{mockup:ui-banner}}: say which side: {{mockup:ui-banner:after}} or {{mockup:ui-banner:before}}.',
        '{{diagram:architecture-system}}: put this token on a line of its own.',
        'Unknown token: {{nonsense}}.',
        'Unknown token: {{ diagram:architecture-system }}.',
        'Unknown token: {{Diagram:architecture-system}}.',
        'Unknown token: {{ that never closes.',
      ],
    });
  });

  it("doesn't depend on the shared pattern's position", () => {
    TOKEN_PATTERN.lastIndex = 10;
    expect(expandTokens('{{steps:flows-checkout}}', ctx)).toMatchObject({ ok: true, markdown: '1. Opens checkout\n2. Pays\n   - System: Charges the card.' });
    TOKEN_PATTERN.lastIndex = 0;
  });

  it('lists the tokens a finalizer may use', () => {
    expect(availableTokens(ITEMS, TYPES)).toEqual([
      '{{diagram:architecture-system}}: the Mermaid flowchart of "System overview".',
      '{{schema:database-restock}}: the schema diff of "RestockReminder table".',
      '{{migration:database-restock}}: the migration notes of "RestockReminder table".',
      '{{mockup:ui-account:after}}: a link to the after mockup of "Account page", copied into the assets folder.',
      '{{mockup:ui-account:before}}: a link to the before mockup of "Account page", copied into the assets folder.',
      '{{mockup:ui-banner:after}}: a link to the after mockup of "Reminder banner", copied into the assets folder.',
      '{{sequence:flows-restock}}: the Mermaid sequence diagram of "Change the lead time".',
      '{{steps:flows-restock}}: the numbered steps of "Change the lead time".',
      '{{steps:flows-checkout}}: the numbered steps of "Checkout".',
      '{{sequence:flows-sync}}: the Mermaid sequence diagram of "Nightly sync".',
    ]);
  });
});
